{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a5de18e8326611e83","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"You are RED-PEN, an adversarial reviewer. Repo: /home/kali/sigil (branch phase8-ws-g-delegate). Your job: BREAK the newly-built DELEGATE web-actor (SIGIL Phase 8 WS-G) and produce concrete, reproducible objections. This is the highest-risk stream (credentials + outbound web actions), so be ruthless. Read-only — object, do not fix.\n\nFILES TO ATTACK (the whole diff of this commit — `git show 2b713a0 --stat` and `git show 2b713a0`):\n- sigil/agents/actor.py  (the DELEGATE agent — plan→preview→approve→execute→verify)\n- sigil/agents/vault.py  (CredentialVault — password off-spine)\n- sigil/agents/actor_gate.py  (action_token + action_approved)\n- sigil/agents/actor_scope.py  (origin allowlist + creation cap)\n- sigil/agents/web_engine.py  (HttpEngine/FakeEngine, detect_block, needs_js)\n- sigil/governor/promotion.py (DELEGATE added to NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS)\n- tests/test_actor.py\n\nCONTEXT you must load first: read sigil/agents/operator.py (the transaction pattern this mirrors), sigil/agents/approvals.py (verify_approval / ApprovalQueue), sigil/agents/egress.py or perception/egress.py (the egress-token pattern actor_gate mirrors), sigil/agents/sources.py (fetch_raw, _vetted_ip, is_public_host, _PinnedHTTP(S)Handler, _NoRedirect), sigil/mesh/__init__.py (authorized_devices), sigil/agents/base.py (Agent._dispatch, governor gate).\n\nTHE DOCTRINE THIS MUST UPHOLD — try to violate EACH with a concrete repro (exact inputs → wrong outcome):\n1. CREDENTIAL NEVER ON SPINE/LOG/JOURNAL/UA/REFERER. Trace every place a resolved password/email/username can flow. Can a password reach: the append-only spine JSONL bytes? the 0700 step journal file? a log line? a Referer/UA header? an exception/traceback? the action_token preimage? the field_binding? a `refusal`/`finding`/`event` payload? Construct the exact WebStep/vault state that leaks it.\n2. NO ACTION WITHOUT A VERIFIED OWNER APPROVAL. Can execute() ever call engine.act() when action_approved() is false? Any path (missing signal, tampered record, journal swap, wrong seq) that reaches engine.act without a verified, correctly-bound approval? Forge/replay an approval: unsigned, self-signed with a non-owner key, an approval targeting a different seq, an approval whose token differs.\n3. APPROVAL REPLAY across actions/services/pages. The action_token binds service|step_kind|page_sha256|field_binding, and action_approved binds target_seq. Find a case where an approval for step A authorizes step B (same token but different seq? tampered payload? two steps sharing a journal file?).\n4. TOCTOU. execute() re-fetches + re-derives the token. Can a page change OR a credential rotation (vault version bump) OR a literal-field edit slip past the re-derivation and still act with stale/attacker-controlled data? Check that the field_binding is RE-COMPUTED from current vault state at execute (not reused from the stored record). Check the journal is read from an attacker-uncontrollable path.\n5. EVASION DRIFT — the block-check must precede any escalation and NO evasion capability may exist. Confirm: detect_block runs BEFORE any needs_js/browser decision; there is NO solver/proxy/UA-rotation/stealth/retry-with-different-UA path; a 403/429/CAPTCHA STOPS and is surfaced, never bypassed. Is there ANY code path where a block leads to a retry, a browser, or a different request that could defeat it?\n6. THIRD-PARTY ATTACK / SSRF. ActorScope is deny-all origin allowlist + is_public_host. Can origin_allowed be tricked (redirect, relative URL, IDNA/unicode host, userinfo@host, IP-literal, uppercase, trailing dot, port confusion, scheme confusion) into allowing an off-list or private/loopback/metadata host? Does HttpEngine.act independently vet the IP (does _vetted_ip pin the resolved IP, block private ranges)? Can a redirect during fetch reach a private host?\n7. IMPERSONATION — must be impossible by ABSENCE of a code path. Confirm there is no as_identity/impersonate/on_behalf parameter anywhere and no way to resolve fields from anything but the owner's own vault.\n8. MASS CREATION. creation_cap (default 1) checked at preview AND execute. Can the cap be bypassed by: previewing N steps before any execute (all pass the preview check since 0 applied), then approving+executing all N? (This is a REAL suspected hole — check carefully: creation_allowed counts APPLIED account.create records; if you preview 3 account.create steps they ALL queue because none is applied yet, then approve+execute all 3 → 3 accounts despite cap=1. Verify whether execute re-checks the cap. If execute does NOT re-check creation_allowed, this is a CONFIRMED cap bypass.)\n9. TEST HONESTY. Are the tests real, or do any skip-as-pass / assert nothing / green-wash? Does the FakeEngine faithfully model the real one? Is the real-oracle test gated on kernel presence correctly (not passing when the binary is absent because classify fail-closes to A3)?\n\nFor EACH finding: give it a severity, the exact file:line, a concrete repro (the WebStep(s) + vault/store/scope state + the call sequence), the wrong outcome observed vs the doctrine it violates, and the minimal fix direction. If a defense holds, say so briefly and move on. Pay special attention to #8 (creation-cap at preview-only) and #1 (password in any derived string). Verify each objection by actually reading the code path end-to-end; prefer CONFIRMED (you traced it) over PLAUSIBLE. Return a ranked list of concrete objections that should block the merge, or an attestation of what you attacked and why it held."},"uuid":"dbbb6fdc-ffc5-4845-bafd-e299d9ef956d","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:05:22.648Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"dbbb6fdc-ffc5-4845-bafd-e299d9ef956d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a5de18e8326611e83","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"<command-message>crucible</command-message>\n<command-name>crucible</command-name>\n<skill-format>true</skill-format>"},{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main/.claude/skills/crucible\n\n# CRUCIBLE — operating the engine\n\nCRUCIBLE turns an authorized seed URL into **oracle-confirmed** findings, reasons over them,\nand produces auditable reports — inside a fail-closed safety stack. Its whole value is one\nrule; internalize it before anything else:\n\n> **A claim is a FACT only when a deterministic oracle fires over data a real target\n> produced.** You (the LLM) ADVISE where to look and what a result might mean; the oracle\n> CONFIRMS. Nothing else — not your confidence, not a critic's endorsement, not a plausible\n> story — promotes a claim to a fact.\n\nThe standing doctrine every reasoning call operates under is\n[framework/cognitive/metacognition.md](../../../framework/cognitive/metacognition.md) (it is\ninjected into every LLM system prompt). The full architecture — agents, event spine, veracity\nlayer, learning/metacognition core, safety stack — is\n[framework/v2/docs/CRUCIBLE-AI.md](../../../framework/v2/docs/CRUCIBLE-AI.md). The binding\nconstitution is [CLAUDE.md](../../../CLAUDE.md); never relax scope, destruction, evidence, or\nhonesty rules.\n\n## The invariants (never violate these)\n\n1. **Oracle authority.** Only a fired oracle confirms. Critics/RL/reflection/self-consistency\n   may only *advise, re-rank, defer, or abstain* — never promote a finding or override the oracle.\n2. **Coverage.** Never silently skip an authorized attack surface. Learning and reflection\n   *deprioritise* (re-rank / defer); they never gate a surface out.\n3. **Prove by re-execution.** A finding is a fact only if its retained `oracle_context`\n   re-fires. The veracity firewall (`framework/v2/veracity/`) re-runs proofs; it can only demote.\n4. **Non-circular learning.** An EXPLOITABLE label needs ≥2 independent corroborating oracle\n   kinds; a silent oracle is never auto-labelled a false positive.\n5. **Refuse honestly, fail closed.** Decline to conclude what you cannot ground; record every\n   refusal as evidence. Hard limits (scope, authorization, destruction, real user data) are\n   inviolable — when unsure, stop and ask.\n6. **Determinism + append-only.** No wallclock/global-rng in learning/reward math; the event\n   spine is append-only (supersede, never edit).\n\n## Structured workflows\n\n### Run an authorized engagement (end-to-end, gated)\n1. Confirm the active target's `targets/<slug>/charter.md` authorizes it (constitution §II).\n2. `python3 -m framework.v2 engage <slug> <seed-url> [--recon] [--spine]`\n   — crawls, audits, confirms via oracles, chains attack paths, scores confidence, and\n   (with `--spine`) mirrors the whole run onto the immutable blackboard event stream.\n   (`--strict-evidence` is a **`scan`**-only flag, below — not an `engage` flag.)\n3. Every request passes the full gate chain; an out-of-scope seed or tripped kill-switch\n   refuses *before* traffic and is recorded as a `refusal` event.\n\n### Loopback-only quick scan\n`python3 -m framework.v2 scan http://127.0.0.1:<port>/ --format json|sarif|html [--strict-evidence]`\n— the export states each finding's **live grounding** (fact / ungrounded / contradicted);\n`--strict-evidence` withholds non-fact findings from the rendered doc but keeps them in\n`--reverifiable-out`.\n\n### Re-verify a report (prove-don't-guess, offline)\n`python3 -m framework.v2 verify <report.json>` re-runs each finding's retained `oracle_context`.\n`python3 -m framework.v2 evidence ...` builds/verifies signed, hash-linked evidence certificates\n(and, with report claims bound in, fails closed when a report sentence has no backing evidence).\n\n### Inspect / audit the event spine\nThe blackboard (`framework/v2/agents/blackboard.py`) is the one append-only, typed, provenance-\nlinked event stream. Replay it with `Blackboard.replay(engagement=..., since_id=...)`.\nCryptographic tamper-evidence: `framework/v2/agents/spine_chain.py`\n(`build_spine_chain` / `sign_spine_head` / `verify_spine_head`).\n\n### Recon / intel, memory, self-improvement\n`python3 -m framework.v2 intel ...` (OSINT into the shared world-model; gated egress),\n`memory ...` (cross-engagement priors — never fabricate a score), `improve ...`\n(authorise-not-apply — it never self-applies a change).\n\n### Read-only operator console\n`python3 -m framework.v2 console` — loopback, read-only, zero-impact operator UI over the run.\n\n## What NOT to do\n- Do not build detection-evasion / identity-rotation / stay-hidden-from-defenders capability —\n  CRUCIBLE is correlatable and authorized by doctrine (constitution §VI).\n- Do not let any learned/critic/LLM signal enter the deterministic oracle/SCE/calibration inputs\n  or promote a finding.\n- Do not attack third parties (payment/IdP/CDN) — test only the operator's integration with them.\n"}]},"isMeta":true,"uuid":"bf1e6080-3663-4912-84b9-a28b0075b406","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:05:22.672Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

a literal is reduced to a content hash (the value is journaled 0700, never on the spine).\"\"\"\n53\t    if src.startswith(\"literal:\"):\n54\t        return \"literal:\" + sha256_hex(src[len(\"literal:\"):].encode(\"utf-8\"))[:16]\n55\t    return src\n56\t\n57\t\n58\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n59\tclass WebStep:\n60\t    service: str\n61\t    kind: str                                    # navigate|fill|submit|account.create|login|purchase\n62\t    url: str\n63\t    fields: dict = field(default_factory=dict)   # {form_field: \"vault:password\"|\"vault:email\"|\"vault:username\"|\"literal:...\"}\n64\t    # NOTE: there is deliberately NO identity/impersonate field — fields resolve ONLY from the owner's own vault.\n65\t\n66\t\n67\tclass Delegate(Agent):\n68\t    name = \"DELEGATE\"\n69\t    mandate = \"owner-consented identity/account manager + web actor; per-action approved, offense-free\"\n70\t    ceiling = Tier.A2\n71\t\n72\t    def __init__(self, store=None, *, scope: ActorScope, vault=None, engine=None,\n73\t                 classifier=None, trusted_pubkey=None):\n74\t        super().__init__(store)\n75\t        self.scope = scope\n76\t        self._vault = vault\n77\t        self.engine = engine or HttpEngine()\n78\t        self._classifier = classifier\n79\t        self._trusted = trusted_pubkey\n80\t\n81\t    def _vaultobj(self):\n82\t        if self._vault is None:\n83\t            from .vault import CredentialVault\n84\t            self._vault = CredentialVault()\n85\t        return self._vault\n86\t\n87\t    def _cls(self):\n88\t        if self._classifier is None:\n89\t            from .kernel_classify import KernelClassifier\n90\t            self._classifier = KernelClassifier()\n91\t        return self._classifier\n92\t\n93\t    def _tp(self):\n94\t        if self._trusted is None:\n95\t            from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n96\t            self._trusted = owner_pubkey()\n97\t        return self._trusted\n98\t\n99\t    def _field_binding(self, service: str, fields: dict) -> str:\n100\t        \"\"\"A value-free binding: field NAMES + vault-references + literal-CONTENT-HASHES, prefixed with\n101\t        the current vault VERSION when any field references the vault (so a rotation → new token →\n102\t        re-approval). Deterministically reproducible at execute from the journaled fields + vault state.\"\"\"\n103\t        parts, uses_vault = [], False\n104\t        for k in sorted(fields):\n105\t            src = \";\".join(parts)\n110\t        if uses_vault:\n111\t            rec = self._vaultobj().get_record(service)\n112\t            binding = f\"vaultv{rec.version if rec else 0}|{binding}\"\n113\t != ACTION_SIGNAL:\n168\t            res.notes.append(f\"seq {step_seq} is not a web-actor step\")\n169\t            return res\n170\t        token = rec.payload[\"action_token\"]\n171\t        if not action_approved(self.store, step_seq, token, self._tp()):\n172\t            res.notes.append(f\"step {step_seq} has no verified owner approval — NO request made\")\n173\t            return res\n174\t json.loads(Path(rec.payload[\"journal\"]).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n176\t            service, kind, url, fields = j[\"service\"], j[\"kind\"], j[\"url\"], j[\"fields\"]\n177\t        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):\n178\t            res.notes.append(\"step journal missing/unreadable — cannot execute\")\n179\t            return res\n180\t        pv = self.store.append(kind=\"finding\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n184\t                              payload={\"signal\": \"web.actor.blocked\", \"decision\": \"surfaced\", \"url\": url,\n185\t action\"})\n187\t            res.notes.append(f\"BLOCKED at execute {url}: {block} — surfaced, no action\")\n188\t            return res\n189\t        # RE-DERIVE the token from the CURRENT page hash + CURRENT vault version + journaled fields; a\n190\t        # page change OR a credential rotation OR a journal edit → mismatch → abort (never trust a field).\n191\t        fresh = A `VaultRecord` has NO `password` field — only a `password_ref`\n4\t(a keyring key name); the password lives in the OS keyring. The manifest (email/username/ref/version)\n5\tlives in a 0700 dir OFF the append-only spine. The password is resolved from the keyring ONLY at\n6\texecute time, into a local variable — never assigned to a Proposal payload, never logged, never\n7\tjournaled. `version` bumps on every edit and BINDS an approval (rotate → version bump → re-approval),\n8\tso the spine binds by `service+vault_ref+version` — deliberately NOT a hash of the value (hashing a\n9\tlow-entropy identity field onto an append-only log is itself a weak-preimage leak).\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport json\n13\timport os\n14\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n15\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n18\t\n19\t_VAULT = SIGIL_HOME / \"vault\"\n20\t_MANIFEST = _VAULT / \"manifest.json\"\n21\t\n22\t\n23\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n24\tclass VaultRecord:\n25\t    service: str\n26\t    email: str = \"\"\n27\t    username: str = \"\"\n28\t    password_ref: str = \"\"            # keyring key name — NOT the password\n29\t    notes: str = \"\"\n30\t    version: int = 1\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tclass CredentialVault:\n34\t    def __init__(self, secret_store=None):\n35\t        from ..platform.secrets import SecretStore\n36\t        self.secrets = secret_store or SecretStore()\n37\t\n38\t    def _load(self) -> dict:\n39\t        try:\n40\t            return json.loads(_MANIFEST.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n41\t        except (OSError, ValueError):\n42\t            return {}\n43\t\n44\t    def _save(self, data: dict) -> None:\n45\t        _VAULT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n46\t        try:\n47\t            os.chmod(_VAULT, 0o700)\n48\t        except OSError:\n49\t            pass\n50\t        fd = os.open(str(_MANIFEST), os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)  # 0600 up-front\n51\t        with os.fdopen(fd, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n52\t            json.dump(data, f)\n53\t\n54\t    def set_record(self, service: str, *, email: str = \"\", username: str = \"\",\n55\t                   password: Optional[str] = None, notes: str = \"\") -> VaultRecord:\n56\t        ref = f\"vault/{service}/password\"\n57\t        if password is not None:\n58\t            self.secrets.set(ref, password)          # → keyring (or 0600 sigil.env), never the spine\n59\t        data = self._load()\n60\t        prev = data.get(service, {})\n61\t        rec = {\"service\": service, \"email\": email or prev.get(\"email\", \"\"),\n62\t               \"username\": username or prev.get(\"username\", \"\"), \"password_ref\": ref,\n63\t               \"notes\": notes or prev.get(\"notes\", \"\"), \"version\": int(prev.get(\"version\", 0)) + 1}\n64\t        data[service] = rec\n65\t        self._save(data)\n66\t        return VaultRecord(**rec)\n67\t\n68\t    def get_record(self, service: str) -> Optional[VaultRecord]:\n69\t        d = self._load().get(service)\n70\t        return VaultRecord(**d) if d else None\n71\t\n72\t    def records(self) -> List[VaultRecord]:\n73\t        return [VaultRecord(**d) for d in self._load().values()]\n74\t\n75\t    def resolve_password(self, service: str) -> Optional[str]:\n76\t        \"\"\"Fetch the password from the keyring — call ONLY at execute-time, into a local var.\"\"\"\n77\t        rec = self.get_record(service)\n78\t        return self.secrets.get(rec.password_ref) if rec else None\n79\t"}]},"uuid":"f12bc3b8-c0fd-43c7-b6ff-a4a8a3d091b5","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:05:53.280Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c2d1fd63-e815-494d-aa9f-6087eddb6884","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"6067670b-039e-47be-926c-bff30098cee4","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a5de18e8326611e83","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018vGnwd2MgDePrz8Mxbd7zT","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Action gate (Phase 8, WS-G G-iii) — a near-verbatim mirror of `perception/egress.py`, but for web\n2\tACTIONS. `action_token = sha256(service|step|page_sha256|field_binding)` binds an approval to ONE\n3\texact action (a page-change or a vault-version bump changes the token → re-approval). `action_approved`\n4\trequires a VERIFIED owner/authorized-device approval whose signed `target_seq` IS that step — a\n5\treplay onto a different action, a wrong token, an unsigned/denied approval → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n7\t\n8\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n9\tfrom .approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n10\tfrom .approvals import verify_approval\n11\t\n12\tACTION_SIGNAL = \"web.actor.step\"\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tdef action_token(service: str, step_kind: str, page_sha256: str, field_binding: str) -> str:\n16\t    return sha256_hex(f\"{service}|{step_kind}|{page_sha256}|{field_binding}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tdef action_approved(store, seq: int, token: str, trusted_pubkey) -> bool:\n20\t    rec = store.get(seq)\n21\t    if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != ACTION_SIGNAL or rec.payload.get(\"action_token\") != token:\n22\t        return False\n23\t    from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n24\t    devices = authorized_devices(store, trusted_pubkey)\n25\t    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=seq):\n26\t        p = r.payload\n27\t        if (p.get(\"signal\") == _APPROVAL_SIGNAL and p.get(\"target_seq\") == seq\n28\t                and p.get(\"approval\") == \"approved\" and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=devices)):\n29\t            return True\n30\t    return False\n31\t"}]},"uuid":"121e6c85-6a4a-457e-bb57-7821b6946af1","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:05:58.543Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6067670b-039e-47be-926c-bff30098cee4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"419ca05f-b24e-4c9a-8f84-5abd7373f0cf","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a5de18e8326611e83","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BucAXm2Ufe66mGo7pdRphY","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"ActorScope (Phase 8, WS-G G-iv) — the origin allowlist that bounds where DELEGATE may act (the\n2\towner's OWN service integrations), mirroring `OperatorScope`: EMPTY = deny-all. A step's origin must\n3\tbe (1) http/https, (2) public (`sources.is_public_host` — no internal hosts), (3) in the allowlist.\n4\tPlus a per-service `creation_cap` (default 1) — mass account creation is out of doctrine; the cap is\n5\tchecked at preview AND execute.\"\"\"\n6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n7\t\n8\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n9\tfrom urllib.parse import urlsplit\n10\t\n11\tfrom .sources import is_public_host\n12\t\n13\t\n14\tdef _origin(url: str) -> Optional[str]:\n15\t    p = urlsplit(url if \"//\" in url else \"https://\" + url)\n16\t    if p.scheme not in (\"http\", \"https\") or not p.hostname:\n17\t        return None\n18\t    return f\"{p.scheme}://{p.hostname.lower()}\"\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tclass ActorScope:\n22\t    def __init__(self, allowed_origins: Optional[List[str]] = None, *, creation_cap: int = 1):\n23\t        self.allowed = {o for o in (_origin(a) for a in (allowed_origins or [])) if o}\n24\t        self.creation_cap = creation_cap\n25\t\n26\t    def origin_allowed(self, url: str) -> bool:\n27\t        p = urlsplit(url)\n28\t        if p.scheme not in (\"http\", \"https\") or not p.hostname or not is_public_host(p.hostname):\n29\t            return False\n30\t        return bool(self.allowed) and f\"{p.scheme}://{p.hostname.lower()}\" in self.allowed\n31\t\n32\t    def creation_allowed(self, store, service: str) -> bool:\n33\t        n = sum(1 for r in store.iter_records()\n34\t                if r.payload.get(\"signal\") == \"web.actor.step\"\n35\t                and r.payload.get(\"step_kind\") == \"account.create\"\n36\t                and r.payload.get(\"service\") == service\n37\t                and r.payload.get(\"status\") == \"applied\")\n38\t        return n < self.creation_cap\n39\t"}]},"uuid":"2891b0b8-fe4f-4fd5-b6bb-e81df61f814b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:03.235Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"419ca05f-b24e-4c9a-8f84-5abd7373f0cf","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"ef5c1384-d6e6-4755-831e-e6b224aaf7cb","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a5de18e8326611e83","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAHLUycLfK3FAT84H5z1","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01RHLRGZBFgpYMJeTosj3LUd","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/web_engine.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":831,"cache_read_input_tokens":35236,"output_tokens":1572,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":831},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1572,"cache_read_input_tokens":35236,"cache_creation_input_tokens":831,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":831,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAHLT1YA8dDMHpQMpqNM","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"cb26acf0-027c-4485-9b51-f06711ba226e","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:29.710Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"cb26acf0-027c-4485-9b51-f06711ba226e","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a5de18e8326611e83","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01RHLRGZBFgpYMJeTosj3LUd","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Web engine (Phase 8, WS-G G-iii) — HTTP-first, headless-browser fallback. `HttpEngine` reuses the\n2\tSSRF-gated, IP-pinned, correlatable-UA `sources.fetch_raw` for the light path. `detect_block` flags a\n3\tCAPTCHA / 403 / 429 / CF-challenge and is checked BEFORE the escalation decision, so \"use the browser\n4\tto defeat a block\" is structurally unreachable. `BrowserEngine` (lazy Playwright, locked-down, no\n5\tstealth/proxy/UA-rotation) is a documented off-by-default seam. `FakeEngine` is the deterministic\n6\tdouble + call spy for tests.\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\timport re\n10\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n11\tfrom typing import Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n12\t\n13\t_BLOCK = re.compile(r\"recaptcha|hcaptcha|turnstile|cf-chl|just a moment|attention required|\\bcaptcha\\b\", re.I)\n14\t_JS_APP = re.compile(r'id=[\"\\'](root|app|__next)[\"\\']', re.I)\n15\t\n16\t\n17\t@dataclass\n18\tclass PageView:\n19\t    ok: bool\n20\t    status: int\n21\t    html: str\n22\t    url: str\n23\t\n24\t\n25\t@runtime_checkable\n26\tclass WebEngine(Protocol):\n27\t    egresses: bool\n28\t    def fetch(self, url: str) -> PageView: ...\n29\t    def act(self, step: dict, resolved: dict) -> dict: ...\n30\t\n31\t\n32\tdef detect_block(status: int, html: str) -> Optional[str]:\n33\t    \"\"\"A block signal (STOP + surface as a positive control — never defeated). Runs BEFORE escalation.\"\"\"\n34\t    if status in (403, 429):\n35\t        return f\"http-{status}\"\n36\t    if _BLOCK.search(html or \"\"):\n37\t        return \"captcha/anti-automation\"\n38\t    return None\n39\t\n40\t\n41\tdef needs_js(html: str, *, min_text: int = 200) -> bool:\n42\t    \"\"\"A MISSING-CAPABILITY signal (escalate to the browser) — deliberately DISJOINT from a block.\"\"\"\n43\t    from .sources import _strip_html\n44\t    return len(_strip_html(html or \"\").strip()) < min_text and bool(_JS_APP.search(html or \"\"))\n45\t\n46\t\n47\tclass HttpEngine:\n48\t    egresses = True\n49\t\n50\t    def fetch(self, url: str) -> PageView:\n51\t        from .sources import fetch_raw\n52\t        r = fetch_raw(url)\n53\t        return PageView(r.ok, r.status, r.raw, url)\n54\t\n55\t    def act(self, step: dict, resolved: dict) -> dict:\n56\t        \"\"\"A simple static-form submit (urlencoded POST). Credentials arrive in `resolved` at the last\n57\t        instant and go ONLY into the POST body — never logged/returned.\"\"\"\n58\t        import urllib.parse\n59\t        import urllib.request\n60\t        from .sources import UA, _NoRedirect, _PinnedHTTPHandler, _PinnedHTTPSHandler, _vetted_ip\n61\t        from urllib.parse import urlsplit\n62\t        url = step.get(\"url\", \"\")\n63\t        ip = _vetted_ip(urlsplit(url).hostname or \"\")\n64\t        if ip is None:\n65\t            return {\"ok\": False, \"reason\": \"ssrf-refused\"}\n66\t        data = urllib.parse.urlencode(resolved).encode(\"utf-8\")\n67\t        opener = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}), _PinnedHTTPHandler(ip),\n68\t                                             _PinnedHTTPSHandler(ip), _NoRedirect)\n69\t        req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, method=\"POST\",\n70\t                                     headers={\"User-Agent\": UA, \"Content-Type\": \"application/x-www-form-urlencoded\"})\n71\t        try:\n72\t            with opener.open(req, timeout=20) as r:\n73\t                return {\"ok\": 200 <= getattr(r, \"status\", 200) < 400, \"status\": getattr(r, \"status\", 200)}\n74\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001\n75\t            return {\"ok\": False, \"reason\": f\"{type(e).__name__}\"}\n76\t\n77\t\n78\tclass FakeEngine:\n79\t    \"\"\"Deterministic double + spy: `pages` = {url: (status, html)}; records every fetch/act.\"\"\"\n80\t    egresses = True\n81\t\n82\t    def __init__(self, pages: Optional[dict] = None):\n83\t        self.pages = pages or {}\n84\t        self.calls: list = []\n85\t\n86\t    def fetch(self, url: str) -> PageView:\n87\t        self.calls.append((\"fetch\", url))\n88\t        st, html = self.pages.get(url, (404, \"\"))\n89\t        return PageView(200 <= st < 300, st, html, url)\n90\t\n91\t    def act(self, step: dict, resolved: dict) -> dict:\n92\t        self.calls.append((\"act\", step.get(\"url\"), sorted(resolved.keys())))   # keys only — never values\n93\t        return {\"ok\": True, \"status\": 200}\n94\t"}]},"uuid":"210c28cb-e003-40c6-952f-0ba1e4a3b495","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:29.717Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cb26acf0-027c-4485-9b51-f06711ba226e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"eded2103-0d47-4045-9341-105b0f54fde5","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a5de18e8326611e83","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01LjRYPt1zYHvS1YVDn24oE5","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Approval queue (SIGIL §5, §9.3 mobile bridge) — the human gate for A2/A3 proposals. An agent\n2\tQUEUES a proposal (governor → QUEUE); the owner APPROVES or DENIES it here. Authentication is an\n3\tEd25519 signature over the canonical (target, decision, approver) by the OWNER key.\n4\t\n5\tRED-PEN HARDENING (Phase 6):\n6\t • The trusted pubkey is the PERSISTED owner identity — NOT the key supplied to the queue, so an\n7\t   attacker cannot self-certify by bringing their own key (RP-APPROVAL-2). approve/deny require the\n8\t   supplied signing key to BE the trusted owner key.\n9\t • `pending()` treats a queued item as resolved ONLY when a superseding approval VERIFIES against the\n10\t   trusted owner key, and keys resolution off the SIGNED target_seq (not the raw supersedes_id) — so\n11\t   an unsigned/forged approval can't silently drop an item (RP-1/RP-APPROVAL-1), and a genuine\n12\t   approval of one item can't be replayed onto another (RP-APPROVAL-4). `verify_approval` is thereby\n13\t   on the enforcement path (RP-APPROVAL-3).\n14\t\n15\tThis records the DECISION; it executes no external effect (SIGIL has no send/deploy path). The\n16\ttransport (Telegram/WhatsApp over WireGuard, §9.3) is a seam over this authenticated core.\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n20\t\n21\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sha256_hex, sign, verify_one\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tSIGNAL = \"governor.approval\"\n25\t_QUEUED_STATUS = \"awaiting-approval\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef _approval_message(target_seq, decision: str, approver: str) -> bytes:\n29\t    m = canonical_json({\"target\": target_seq, \"decision\": decision, \"approver\": approver})\n30\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef verify_approval(record, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str], *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> bool:\n34\t    \"\"\"True iff the approval carries a valid signature over its (target, decision, approver) by the\n35\t    OWNER key OR by a currently-authorized DEVICE key (`extra_pubkeys`). The claimed `pubkey` must be\n36\t    in the allowed set — which is composed ONLY of owner-minted keys (the owner identity + the\n37\t    owner-signed device-authorization ledger, see `sigil.mesh`) — so a key the requester merely\n38\t    presents is never trusted (RP-APPROVAL-2). The signed `target_seq` still binds (RP-APPROVAL-4).\n39\t    Unsigned / not-in-allowed-set / no-trusted-key → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n40\t    p = record.payload if hasattr(record, \"payload\") else record\n41\t    sig, pub = p.get(\"sig\"), p.get(\"pubkey\")\n42\t    allowed = set()\n43\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64:\n44\t        allowed.add(trusted_pubkey_b64)\n45\t    if extra_pubkeys:\n46\t        allowed.update(extra_pubkeys)\n47\t    if not sig or not pub or pub not in allowed:\n48\t        return False\n49\t    msg = _approval_message(p.get(\"target_seq\"), p.get(\"approval\"), p.get(\"approver\"))\n50\t    return verify_one(pub, msg, sig)                # verify against the (owner-minted) claimed key\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef pending(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None, *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> List:\n54\t    \"\"\"Queued proposals with NO valid approval yet, oldest first. Resolution requires a VERIFIED\n55\t    owner/authorized-device approval; an unsigned/forged/unauthorized approval leaves the item\n56\t    pending (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n57\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64 is None:\n58\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n59\t        trusted_pubkey_b64 = owner_pubkey()\n60\t    resolved = set()\n61\t    queued = {}\n62\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n63\t        p = r.payload\n64\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == SIGNAL and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey_b64, extra_pubkeys=extra_pubkeys):\n65\t            resolved.add(p.get(\"target_seq\"))       # honor the SIGNED target, not raw supersedes_id\n66\t        if p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == _QUEUED_STATUS:\n67\t            queued[r.seq] = r\n68\t    return [r for seq, r in sorted(queued.items()) if seq not in resolved]\n69\t\n70\t\n71\tclass ApprovalError(Exception):\n72\t    pass\n73\t\n74\t\n75\tclass ApprovalQueue:\n76\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, owner_key=None,\n77\t                 trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None):\n78\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n79\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n80\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n81\t        # PINNED to the persisted owner identity — never defaulted to the supplied key (RP-APPROVAL-2)\n82\t        self.trusted_pubkey_b64 = trusted_pubkey_b64 if trusted_pubkey_b64 is not None else owner_pubkey()\n83\t\n84\t    def _target(self, seq: int):\n85\t        for r in pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n86\t            if r.seq == seq:\n87\t                return r\n88\t        raise ApprovalError(f\"seq {seq} is not a pending approval (already decided, or not queued)\")\n89\t\n90\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, *, approver: str, reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n91\t        target = self._target(seq)\n92\t        # the signing key MUST be the trusted owner key — not merely \"some key present\" (RP-APPROVAL-2).\n93\t        if (self.owner_key is None or self.trusted_pubkey_b64 is None\n94\t                or self.owner_key.public_key_b64 != self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n95\t            raise ApprovalError(\"approval requires the OWNER's signing key (missing or not the trusted key)\")\n96\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n97\t        sig = sign(self.owner_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n98\t        payload = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq, \"target_kind\": target.kind,\n99\t                   \"target_tier\": target.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason,\n100\t                   \"pubkey\": self.trusted_pubkey_b64, \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg),\n101\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n102\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n103\t                                 payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n104\t\n105\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n106\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n107\t\n108\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n109\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n110\t"}]},"uuid":"9b904767-6816-4234-8b64-06004c2ca11f","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:33.325Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"eded2103-0d47-4045-9341-105b0f54fde5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Returns (restored, skip_reason). `verify_unchanged` (undo) refuses\n346\t        to clobber a file the owner changed since (hash-bind), and re-resolves the path through scope.\"\"\"\n347\t        path = inv.get(\"path\")\n348\t        if verify_unchanged:\n349\t            if path is None or self.scope.resolve(path, \"read\") is None:\n350\t                return False, f\"{path}: left scope — not restored\"\n351\t            cur = Path(path)\n352\t            cur_hash = _hash_bytes(cur.read_bytes()) if cur.exists() else None\n353\t            if inv.get(\"post_hash\") is not None and cur_hash != inv.get(\"post_hash\"):\n354\t                return False, f\"{path}: changed since execute — NOT clobbered\"\n355\t            if inv.get(\"op\") == \"delete-created\" and cur_hash != inv.get(\"post_hash\"):\n356\t                return False, f\"{path}: changed since execute — not deleted\"\n357\t        try:\n358\t            if inv[\"op\"] == \"restore\":\n359\t                shutil.copyfile(inv[\"pre\"], path)\n360\t                if inv.get(\"pre_mode\") is not None:\n361\t                    os.chmod(path, inv[\"pre_mode\"])                 # BLOCK-3: restore original mode\n362\t            elif inv[\"op\"] == \"delete-created\":\n363\t                Path(path).unlink(missing_ok=True)\n364\t            else:\n365\t                return False, None                                  # irreversible — handled by caller\n366\t            return True, None\n367\t        except OSError as e:\n368\t            return False, f\"{path}: restore FAILED ({e})\"\n369\t\n370\t    def _rollback(self, applied: List[dict], *, verify_unchanged: bool = False) -> tuple[int, str]:\n371\t        n, failed, irreversible = 0, [], []\n372\t        for inv in reversed(applied):\n373\t            if inv[\"op\"] == \"irreversible\":\n374\t                irreversible.append(inv.get(\"step\"))\n375\t                continue\n376\t            ok, why = self._restore_one(inv, verify_unchanged=verify_unchanged)\n377\t            if ok:\n378\t                n += 1\n379\t            elif why:\n380\t                failed.append(why)                                  # BLOCK-5: never call a failed restore clean\n381\t        parts = []\n382\t        if failed:\n383\t            parts.append(\"PARTIAL — not restored: \" + \"; \".join(failed))\n384\t        if irreversible:\n385\t            parts.append(f\"step(s) {irreversible} IRREVERSIBLE (shell already ran)\")\n386\t        return n, (\"; \".join(parts) if parts else \"clean rollback\")\n387\t\n388\t    def undo(self, execute_seq: int) -> AgentResult:\n389\t        rec = self.store.get(execute_seq)\n390\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n391\t        if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != \"operator.execute\" or rec.payload.get(\"status\") != \"APPLIED\":\n392\t            res.notes.append(f\"seq {execute_seq} is not an applied operator execution\")\n393\t            return res\n394\t        # BLOCK-6: single-shot — refuse a second undo of the same execution.\n395\t        for r in self.store.iter_records(since_seq=execute_seq):\n396\t            if r.payload.get(\"signal\") == \"operator.undo\" and r.payload.get(\"target_seq\") == execute_seq:\n397\t                res.notes.append(f\"execute {execute_seq} was already undone (seq {r.seq}) — refusing a replay\")\n398\t                return res\n399\t        n, note = self._rollback(rec.payload.get(\"inverses\", []), verify_unchanged=True)  # A\n9\trefused/unreadable source is simply empty evidence, never a fabricated one.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport http.client\n13\timport ipaddress\n14\timport re\n15\timport socket\n16\timport urllib.error\n17\timport urllib.request\n18\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n19\tfrom pathlib import Path\n20\tfrom typing import Optional\n21\tfrom urllib.parse import urlsplit\n22\t\n23\t# One stable, CORRELATABLE User-Agent (OBSIDIAN OPSEC §VI: identifiable, NOT evasive — never rotated).\n24\tUA = \"SIGIL-SCHOLAR/1.0 (authorized owner research)\"\n25\t\n26\t_TAG = re.compile(r\"<[^>]+>\")\n27\t_WS = re.compile(r\"\\s+\")\n28\t_SCRIPT = re.compile(r\"<(script|style)[^>]*>.*?</\\1>\", re.S | re.I)\n29\t\n30\t\n31\tdef _strip_html(html: str) -> str:\n32\t    html = _SCRIPT.sub(\" \", html)\n33\t    return _WS.sub(\" \", _TAG.sub(\" \", html)).strip()\n34\t\n35\t\n36\tdef _vetted_ip(host: str) -> Optional[str]:\n37\t    \"\"\"Resolve `host`; return ONE address to pin iff EVERY resolved address is public unicast, else\n38\t    None. Fail-closed: an unresolvable host, or any private/loopback/link-local/reserved/multicast/\n39\t    unspecified/IPv4-mapped-internal address, yields None.\"\"\"\n40\t    if not host:\n41\t        return None\n42\t    try:\n43\t        infos = socket.getaddrinfo(host, None)\n44\t    except (socket.gaierror, UnicodeError, OSError):\n45\t        return None\n46\t    if not infos:\n47\t        return None\n48\t    chosen = None\n49\t    for family, _t, _p, _c, sockaddr in infos:\n50\t        try:\n51\t            ip = ipaddress.ip_address(sockaddr[0])\n52\t        except ValueError:\n53\t            return None\n54\t        # unwrap IPv4-mapped/compat IPv6 (::ffff:127.0.0.1) so an internal v4 can't hide as v6\n55\t        if isinstance(ip, ipaddress.IPv6Address) and ip.ipv4_mapped is not None:\n56\t            ip = ip.ipv4_mapped\n57\t        # Reject anything NOT globally routable — `not is_global` also closes RFC 6598 CGNAT/shared\n58\t        # space 100.64.0.0/10 (incl. the Alibaba metadata IP 100.100.100.200) and other non-global\n59\t        # ranges the explicit list misses (red-pen BLOCK-1). return self.do_open(factory, req, context=self._context)\n124\t\n125\t\n126\tclass _NoRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):\n127\t    \"\"\"Refuse to follow 3xx — a public URL must not A\n148\t    non-public/unresolvable host → ok=False, status=0 (no request leaves the machine).\"\"\"\n149\t    if not ref.startswith((\"http://\", \"https://\")):\n150\t        return FetchResult(False, 0, \"\", ref, reason=\"not-http\")\n151\t    host = urlsplit(ref).hostname or \"\"\n152\t    ip = _vetted_ip(host)\n153\t    if ip is None:\n154\t        return FetchResult(False, 0, \"\", ref, reason=\"ssrf-refused\")\n155\t    # ProxyHandler({}) disables env http_proxy/https_proxy so routing can't be diverted (N3); the\n156\t    # pinned handlers force the socket to the vetted IP regardless.\n157\t    opener = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}),\n158\t                                         _PinnedHTTPHandler(ip), _PinnedHTTPSHandler(ip), _NoRedirect)\n159\t    req = urllib.request.Request(ref, headers={\"User-Agent\": UA})\n160\t    try:\n161\t        with opener.open(req, timeout=timeout) as r:\n162\t            raw = r.read(max_bytes).decode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\")\n163\t            return FetchResult(True, getattr(r, \"status\", 200), raw, ref, headers=dict(r.headers))\n164\t    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:                 # 3xx-not-followed / 4xx / 5xx — surface the code\n165\t        body = \"\"\n166\t        try:\n167\t            body = e.read(max_bytes).decode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\")\n168\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n169\t            pass\n170\t        return FetchResult(False, e.code, body, ref, headers=dict(e.headers or {}), reason=f\"http-{e.code}\")\n171\t    except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError, ValueError) as e:\n172\t        return FetchResult(False, 0, \"\", ref, reason=f\"neterr:{type(e).__name__}\")\n173\t\n174\t\n175\tdef read_source(ref: str, *, timeout: int = 20, max_chars: int = 20000) -> str:\n176\t    \"\"\"Return the text of a source. `http(s)://…` is SSRF-gated (via `fetch_raw`) then HTML-stripped;\n177\t    anything else is a local path. Never raises — returns '' on failure or refusal.\"\"\"\n178\t    if ref.startswith((\"http://\", \"https://\")):\n179\t        res = fetch_raw(ref, timeout=timeout)\n180\t        return _strip_html(res.raw)[:max_chars] if res.ok else \"\"\n181\t    try:\n182\t        return Path(ref).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"ignore\")[:max_chars]\n183\t    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — an unreadable source is simply empty evidence\n184\t        return \"\"\n185\t"}]},"uuid":"bef63ce9-2fc8-4036-8503-1c2a76792496","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:07:05.424Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2e59323d-2de5-4490-937a-7fc19def1756","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Host\ncapabilities and phone/device authorizations are owner-SIGNED spine records, so the mesh topology\nis itself provable, tamper-evident memory; compute (vision/VLM) can run on the best-capable online\nhost, but authority still flows through the one signed spine + WARDEN. Offense-free.\"\"\"\nfrom ..reuse import assert_no_offense\n\nassert_no_offense()\n\nfrom .registry import (  # noqa: E402\n    DeviceApprover,\n    advertise_capability,\n    authorize_device,\n    authorized_devices,\n    capability_map,\n    revoke_device,\n)\n\n__all__ = [\"advertise_capability\", \"capability_map\", \"authorize_device\", \"revoke_device\",\n           \"authorized_devices\", \"DeviceApprover\"]","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"a6083a70-8942-438b-8cad-12d954f47c16","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:07:13.805Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6618b347-3c2f-42df-a9d8-09a31005f600","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"43ac7b1e-5aa1-4bad-a32d-1122e161ae8f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a5de18e8326611e83","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PzEvbj3AG2dkmnkU7fNb2z","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Frontier-vision EGRESS gate (Phase 7, WS-A V-ii). Sending a screen/camera frame to the frontier\n2\tAnthropic VLM uploads private bytes off the owned machine — that is A2 data-egress, not a free\n3\t\"try local then frontier\" quality bump. This gate makes the cascade doctrine-honest:\n4\t\n5\t  • The tier is DERIVED from the WARDEN oracle (`KernelClassifier` on `vision.frontier.upload` → A2),\n6\t    never self-declared.\n7\t  • Nothing uploads until a VERIFIED owner approval exists that is BOUND to this exact egress — an\n8\t    `egress_token = sha256(frame.sha256 | question)`. An approval of a different egress (a replay)\n9\t    does not match `target_seq` and does not authorize the upload.\n10\t  • Absent approval, the egress is QUEUED (an A2 proposal recorded on the spine) and the frontier\n11\t    reading is WITHHELD; the local (on-box) reading still stands.\n12\t\n13\tReuses the hardened signed `ApprovalQueue` verification (`verify_approval`) — the same fail-closed\n14\towner-key check the mesh already trusts.\"\"\"\n15\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n18\tfrom ..agents.approvals import verify_approval\n19\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n20\t\n21\tEGRESS_SIGNAL = \"vision.egress\"\n22\tFRONTIER_TOOL = \"vision.frontier.upload\"   # classified A2 by the WARDEN oracle (contains \"upload\")\n23\t\n24\t\n25\tdef egress_token(frame_sha256: str, question: str) -> str:\n26\t    \"\"\"A stable id binding an approval to exactly this (frame, question) upload.\"\"\"\n27\t    return sha256_hex(f\"{frame_sha256}|{question or ''}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n28\t\n29\t\n30\tdef egress_approved(store, seq: int, token: str, trusted_pubkey) -> bool:\n31\t    \"\"\"True iff `seq` is an egress request bound to `token` AND carries a VERIFIED approval by the\n32\t    owner OR an owner-authorized device. Fail-closed: wrong token, no approval, denied, or an\n33\t    approval of a different seq → False.\"\"\"\n34\t    rec = store.get(seq)\n35\t    if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != EGRESS_SIGNAL or rec.payload.get(\"egress_token\") != token:\n36\t        return False\n37\t    from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n38\t    devices = authorized_devices(store, trusted_pubkey)\n39\t    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=seq):\n40\t        p = r.payload\n41\t        if (p.get(\"signal\") == _APPROVAL_SIGNAL and p.get(\"target_seq\") == seq\n42\t                and p.get(\"approval\") == \"approved\" and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=devices)):\n43\t            return True\n44\t    return False\n45\t"}]},"uuid":"597c5573-f9da-421f-8d9c-0e828f89739b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:07:17.329Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"43ac7b1e-5aa1-4bad-a32d-1122e161ae8f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"2999e870-b08b-486c-ad51-15abe33ca54f","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a5de18e8326611e83","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAHRwxiJDj5jYg1a8z2v","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01VTfRgTrLg7p8zUrdw9X2UY","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/base.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1130,"cache_read_input_tokens":56882,"output_tokens":130,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1130},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":130,"cache_read_input_tokens":56882,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1130,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1130,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAHRvdbJrfQMQTYgNHb2","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"79c0fdb6-a12b-4100-9d49-c9290881c8d3","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:07:23.161Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"79c0fdb6-a12b-4100-9d49-c9290881c8d3","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a5de18e8326611e83","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VTfRgTrLg7p8zUrdw9X2UY","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The SIGIL agent mesh framework (SIGIL §4/§5). Each agent has a MANDATE and an AUTONOMY\n2\tCEILING (A0–A3); everything it does is a `Proposal` carrying a tier. Proposals at or below the\n3\tauto bar (A1) and at or below the agent's ceiling are AUTO-APPLIED (written to the spine);\n4\tanything higher is QUEUED as a pending record a human must approve — never auto-executed.\n5\t\n6\tThe doctrine is enforced STRUCTURALLY, not by discipline: an agent literally cannot take an\n7\texternal-effect action, because no agent has a send/deploy/spend code path. ENVOY, in\n8\tparticular, drafts only — its ceiling is A2 and there is no method that transmits. Agent output\n9\tis provenance-linked spine records, so a brief or a draft is auditable back to what produced it.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n13\tfrom enum import IntEnum\n14\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n15\t\n16\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n17\t\n18\tAGENTS_SOURCE = \"agent\"\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tclass Tier(IntEnum):\n22\t    \"\"\"Mirror of the WARDEN tiers (SIGIL §5), ordered so `<=` compares autonomy.\"\"\"\n23\t    A0 = 0   # observe / answer\n24\t    A1 = 1   # reversible internal act (write a report/brief/event) — the AUTO bar\n25\t    A2 = 2   # external-visible / semi-reversible (send, calendar write) — QUEUED\n26\t    A3 = 3   # destructive / financial / security — explicit, never auto\n27\t\n28\t    def label(self) -> str:\n29\t        return self.name\n30\t\n31\t\n32\tAUTO_BAR = Tier.A1  # A0/A1 auto-apply; A2/A3 queue for human approval\n33\t\n34\t\n35\t@dataclass\n36\tclass Proposal:\n37\t    \"\"\"One thing an agent wants to do: emit a record (auto) or take a gated action (queued).\"\"\"\n38\t    kind: str                       # spine record kind (event/finding/brief/interaction/draft/...)\n39\t    payload: dict\n40\t    tier: Tier = Tier.A1\n41\t    parent_id: Optional[int] = None\n42\t    supersedes_id: Optional[int] = None   # this record supersedes an earlier one (e.g. a resolution)\n43\t\n44\t\n45\t@dataclass\n46\tclass AgentResult:\n47\t    agent: str\n48\t    applied: List[int] = field(default_factory=list)     # seqs written (auto)\n49\t    queued: List[dict] = field(default_factory=list)      # proposals held for approval\n50\t    notes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tclass Agent:\n54\t    \"\"\"Base agent. Subclasses implement `run()`; the base routes every Proposal through the GOVERNOR\n55\t    (SIGIL §5: kill switch, budgets, promotion — Phase 6) and records the outcome on the spine.\n56\t    Backward compatible: with no kill/cap/promotion the governor reproduces the original gate.\"\"\"\n57\t    name: str = \"AGENT\"\n58\t    mandate: str = \"\"\n59\t    ceiling: Tier = Tier.A1\n60\t\n61\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, governor=None):\n62\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n63\t        if governor is None:\n64\t            from ..governor import Governor\n65\t            governor = Governor(self.store)\n66\t        self.governor = governor\n67\t\n68\t    def run(self) -> AgentResult:\n69\t        raise NotImplementedError\n70\t\n71\t    def _dispatch(self, proposals: List[Proposal]) -> AgentResult:\n72\t        from ..governor import Outcome\n73\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n74\t        for p in proposals:\n75\t            effective = max(p.tier, Tier.A0)\n76\t            # promotion scope = the RECORD KIND (the real action written), not a self-asserted label:\n77\t            # a promotion is \"this agent may auto-approve A2 records of this kind\" (red-pen RP-3).\n78\t            decision = self.governor.decide(agent=self.name, tier=effective,\n79\t                                            ceiling=self.ceiling, scope=p.kind)\n80\t            common = {**p.payload, \"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n81\t                      \"governor\": decision.reason}\n82\t            if decision.outcome == Outcome.AUTO:\n83\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n84\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"auto\"},\n85\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n86\t                res.applied.append(seq)\n87\t            elif decision.outcome == Outcome.QUEUE:\n88\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n89\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"queued\", \"status\": \"awaiting-approval\"},\n90\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n91\t                res.queued.append({\"seq\": seq, \"kind\": p.kind, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n92\t                                   \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\") or p.payload.get(\"to\")})\n93\t            else:  # DENY — kill switch or budget; record the refusal, take no action\n94\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n95\t                                  payload={\"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n96\t                                           \"decision\": \"denied\", \"governor\": decision.reason,\n97\t                                           \"of_kind\": p.kind, \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\")},\n98\t                                  parent_id=p.parent_id)\n99\t                res.notes.append(f\"DENIED {p.kind} ({effective.label()}): {decision.reason}\")\n100\t        return res\n101\t"}]},"uuid":"2e4a2273-d161-451b-8e3b-f5e715592612","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:07:23.181Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"79c0fdb6-a12b-4100-9d49-c9290881c8d3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"2ad62173-5bd3-4cfe-b9dd-8e9271e748ee","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a5de18e8326611e83","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAHSPY6jXeGUDem3MW7C","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013QA7w63ofkpccLnGgvSYMY","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/governor/promotion.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2220,"cache_read_input_tokens":58012,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2220,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAHSNFDLqsaPq5ZxjZdH","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"7bb3c4bc-021d-4a48-b16c-c835b0aa21cc","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:07:31.980Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"7bb3c4bc-021d-4a48-b16c-c835b0aa21cc","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a5de18e8326611e83","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013QA7w63ofkpccLnGgvSYMY","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Per-scope promotion policy (SIGIL §5). An owner may promote a specific (agent, scope) so its A2\n2\tproposals auto-approve instead of queuing — EXCEPT ENVOY, which has NO promotion path (§4.6). A3\n3\tnever promotes.\n4\t\n5\tAUTHENTICATED (Phase 6 red-pen RP-2/RP-4): a grant/revoke is meaningless unless SIGNED by the owner\n6\tkey and verified against the persisted trusted pubkey. `is_promoted` ignores any grant that does not\n7\tverify — so a forged grant written by a prompt-injected agent via `self.store` grants nothing. The\n8\tgate scope is the proposal's RECORD KIND (the real action written), not a self-asserted label\n9\t(RP-3), so a promotion is \"agent X may auto-approve A2 records of kind K.\" \"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom typing import Optional\n13\t\n14\tfrom .authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n15\tfrom .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n16\t\n17\tSIGNAL = \"governor.promotion\"\n18\tNO_PROMOTION_AGENTS = frozenset({\"ENVOY\", \"DELEGATE\"})   # outbound + account actions stay human-gated forever\n19\t_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\", \"agent\", \"scope\")\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tclass PromotionPolicy:\n23\t    def __init__(self, store, *, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n24\t        self.store = store\n25\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n26\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n27\t\n28\t    def grant(self, agent: str, scope: str = \"*\"):\n29\t        if agent in NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS:\n30\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\",\n31\t                              payload={\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"decision\": \"refused\", \"agent\": agent,\n32\t                                       \"scope\": scope, \"tier\": \"A0\",\n33\t                                       \"reason\": f\"{agent} has no promotion path (SIGIL §4.6) — outbound stays human-gated\"})\n34\t            return None\n35\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"granted\", \"agent\": agent, \"scope\": scope}\n36\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": \"owner\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n37\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n38\t\n39\t    def revoke(self, agent: str, scope: str = \"*\") -> int:\n40\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"revoked\", \"agent\": agent, \"scope\": scope}\n41\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": \"owner\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n42\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n43\t\n44\t    def is_promoted(self, agent: str, scope: str = \"*\") -> bool:\n45\t        if agent in NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS:\n46\t            return False                          # never — structural, not policy\n47\t        state: dict = {}\n48\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n49\t            p = r.payload\n50\t            if p.get(\"signal\") != SIGNAL or p.get(\"state\") not in (\"granted\", \"revoked\"):\n51\t                continue\n52\t            if not verify_signed(p, _CORE, self.trusted_pubkey):\n53\t                continue                          # fail-closed: an unsigned/forged grant grants nothing\n54\t            state[(p.get(\"agent\"), p.get(\"scope\"))] = p[\"state\"]   # latest verified wins\n55\t        return state.get((agent, scope)) == \"granted\" or state.get((agent, \"*\")) == \"granted\"\n56\t"}]},"uuid":"a493a284-7fb3-430b-828d-f5b102a34316","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:07:32.028Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7bb3c4bc-021d-4a48-b16c-c835b0aa21cc","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"a7c9f48c-b597-4077-b5fd-79c2d6b4493b","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a5de18e8326611e83","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAHT5nHcaWPbhrHBqP3k","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01TYPXBxgnb3RF4r5AisBuXu","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /home/kali/sigil && sed -n '1,200p' sigil/governor/__init__.py","description":"Read governor decide logic"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1844,"cache_read_input_tokens":60232,"output_tokens":152,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1844},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":152,"cache_read_input_tokens":60232,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1844,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1844,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAHT33qR2p8PxC4XAJyw","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"7fcd0439-d4a3-4b0f-9356-3122ca4f7d4b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:07:39.247Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"7fcd0439-d4a3-4b0f-9356-3122ca4f7d4b","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a5de18e8326611e83","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TYPXBxgnb3RF4r5AisBuXu","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"SIGIL governor (Phase 6, SIGIL §5 mechanics) — the runtime enforcement of the WARDEN autonomy\nmodel for the AGENT MESH (the Rust `kernel/` WARDEN governs kernel-level actions; this is its\nPython-side mirror for agents). Every agent Proposal is decided here BEFORE it is written:\n\n  • KILL SWITCH (sealed latch): when engaged, the mesh is halted — only A0 observe/read survives.\n  • BUDGETS: per-agent daily action/interrupt caps, fail-closed (over budget → deny + log).\n  • PROMOTION: a per-(agent, scope) grant may auto-approve A2 — EXCEPT ENVOY outbound, which has NO\n    promotion path by construction (SIGIL §4.6). A3 never auto-promotes.\n\nAll state lives on the append-only spine (kill/promotion are events; budget spend is derived from\nthe log), so the governor's every decision is itself auditable — which is what makes the acceptance\nbar (\"zero unauthorized A2/A3 in the audit log\") provable rather than promised. Offense-free by\ndoctrine (assert_no_offense).\"\"\"\nfrom ..reuse import assert_no_offense\n\nassert_no_offense()\n\nfrom .budget import BudgetCaps, BudgetLedger  # noqa: E402\nfrom .core import Decision, Governor, Outcome  # noqa: E402\nfrom .killswitch import KillSwitch  # noqa: E402\nfrom .promotion import PromotionPolicy  # noqa: E402\n\n__all__ = [\"Governor\", \"Decision\", \"Outcome\", \"KillSwitch\", \"BudgetCaps\", \"BudgetLedger\",\n           \"PromotionPolicy\"]","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"252b827a-973e-4223-bc7d-61f7ce4e644f","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:07:42.879Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7fcd0439-d4a3-4b0f-9356-3122ca4f7d4b","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"252b827a-973e-4223-bc7d-61f7ce4e644f","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a5de18e8326611e83","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAHToR55qtNhDZZLxZ12","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01JFs4PZXJLgG8P4CUPhQthT","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /home/kali/sigil && sed -n '1,200p' sigil/governor/core.py","description":"Read governor core decide"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":741,"cache_read_input_tokens":62076,"output_tokens":105,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":741},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":105,"cache_read_input_tokens":62076,"cache_creation_input_tokens":741,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":741,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAHTnNZXR7Dgt6DNGt6p","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"07ccae08-4cb2-4c02-993e-8a5f93131de4","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:07:48.160Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"07ccae08-4cb2-4c02-993e-8a5f93131de4","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a5de18e8326611e83","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JFs4PZXJLgG8P4CUPhQthT","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"The Governor (SIGIL §5) — the single decision point the agent dispatch gate consults for EVERY\nproposal. It composes the kill switch, budgets, and promotion policy into one fail-closed verdict:\nAUTO (write it), QUEUE (hold for human approval), or DENY (refuse + log). The order matters and is\ndeliberately conservative: observe is always allowed; a kill halts everything above observe; budget\nis checked before any auto-grant; A2 auto-approves only under an explicit promotion; A3 never autos.\n\nKill/promotion are read FRESH per decision (a mid-run kill halts the rest of the run). Backward\ncompatible: with no kill, no cap, and no promotion, A0/A1≤ceiling → AUTO and A2/A3 → QUEUE, exactly\nas the pre-Phase-6 gate behaved.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom enum import Enum\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom .budget import BudgetCaps, BudgetLedger\nfrom .killswitch import KillSwitch\nfrom .promotion import PromotionPolicy\n\n\nclass Outcome(Enum):\n    AUTO = \"auto\"\n    QUEUE = \"queue\"\n    DENY = \"deny\"\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Decision:\n    outcome: Outcome\n    reason: str\n\n\ndef _utc_day() -> str:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).date().isoformat()   # runtime bucketing only (never digested)\n\n\ndef _load_caps() -> BudgetCaps:\n    \"\"\"Optional per-agent caps from ~/.sigil/budgets.json ({daily_actions, daily_interrupts}).\n    Absent/malformed → uncapped (budgets opt-in; default behavior unchanged).\"\"\"\n    try:\n        import json\n\n        from ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n        f = SIGIL_HOME / \"budgets.json\"\n        if not f.exists():\n            return BudgetCaps()\n        d = json.loads(f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n        return BudgetCaps(daily_actions=d.get(\"daily_actions\"), daily_interrupts=d.get(\"daily_interrupts\"))\n    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):\n        return BudgetCaps()\n\n\nclass Governor:\n    def __init__(self, store, *, caps: Optional[BudgetCaps] = None, day_iso: Optional[str] = None,\n                 owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n        from .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n        self.store = store\n        ok = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n        tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n        self.kill = KillSwitch(store, owner_key=ok, trusted_pubkey=tp)\n        self.promo = PromotionPolicy(store, owner_key=ok, trusted_pubkey=tp)\n        self.budget = BudgetLedger(store, caps if caps is not None else _load_caps())\n        self._day = day_iso\n\n    def decide(self, *, agent: str, tier, ceiling, scope: Optional[str] = None) -> Decision:\n        from ..agents.base import AUTO_BAR, Tier\n        scope = scope or \"*\"\n\n        # A0 observe/read is ALWAYS allowed — even under a kill switch (perception + memory stay alive).\n        if tier <= Tier.A0:\n            return Decision(Outcome.AUTO, \"observe (A0)\")\n\n        # KILL SWITCH: the mesh is halted; nothing above observe may act.\n        if self.kill.is_engaged():\n            return Decision(Outcome.DENY, \"kill switch engaged — agent mesh halted (A0 observe only)\")\n\n        # BUDGET: fail-closed — at cap, deny.\n        over, why = self.budget.over_budget(agent, self._day or _utc_day())\n        if over:\n            return Decision(Outcome.DENY, why)\n\n        # Within the auto bar (A0/A1) and the agent's ceiling → auto, as always.\n        if tier <= AUTO_BAR and tier <= ceiling:\n            return Decision(Outcome.AUTO, f\"within auto bar ({tier.label()})\")\n\n        # A2 may auto-approve ONLY under an explicit per-scope promotion (never for ENVOY).\n        if tier == Tier.A2 and tier <= ceiling and self.promo.is_promoted(agent, scope):\n            return Decision(Outcome.AUTO, f\"A2 auto-approved by promotion ({agent}/{scope})\")\n\n        # Everything else (A2 unpromoted, A3, or above ceiling) queues for explicit human approval.\n        return Decision(Outcome.QUEUE, f\"queued for approval ({tier.label()})\")","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"e7d92174-1a3d-409e-8270-23d7ce96ac7c","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:07:50.544Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"07ccae08-4cb2-4c02-993e-8a5f93131de4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

= \"SENTINEL-PW-9f3a2b-UNIQUE-DO-NOT-LEAK\"\n34\tSVC = \"https://svc.example\"\n35\t\n36\t\n37\tdef _store():\n38\t    return SpineStore(tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\"))\n39\t\n40\t\n41\tclass FakeCls:\n42\t    \"\"\"Deterministic stand-in for the Rust oracle: only a clean read-verb is A0; every actor verb → A3.\"\"\"\n43\t    def classify(self, tool):\n44\t        return Tier.A0 if tool == \"http.get\" else Tier.A3\n45\t\n46\t\n47\tclass FakeVault:\n48\t    \"\"\"In-memory vault double (no keyring, no manifest). `password` is held out-of-band, resolved late.\"\"\"\n49\t    def __init__(self):\n50\t        self._r, self._pw = {}, {}\n51\t\n52\t    def add(self, service, *, email=\"\", username=\"\", password=\"\", version=1):\n53\t        self._r[service] = VaultRecord(service=service, email=email, username=username,\n54\t                                       password_ref=f\"vault/{service}/password\", version=version)\n55\t        self._pw[service] = password\n56\t\n57\t    def get_record(self, service):\n58\t        return self._r.get(service)\n59\t\n60\t    def resolve_password(self, service):\n61\t        return self._pw.get(service)\n62\t\n63\t    def bump(self, service):                          # simulate a credential rotation (version++)\n64\t        r = self._r[service]\n65\t        self._r[service] = VaultRecord(service=r.service, email=r.email, username=r.username,\n66\t                                       password_ref=r.password_ref, version=r.version + 1)\n67\t\n68\t\n69\tdef _vault():\n70\t    v = FakeVault()\n71\t    v.add(\"svc\", email=\"me@owner.example\", username=\"owner\", password=SENTINEL)\n72\t    return v\n73\t\n74\t\n75\tdef _delegate(store, *, pages=None, allowed=(SVC,), cap=1, vault=None):\n76\t    engine = FakeEngine(pages if pages is not None else {f\"{SVC}/signup\": (200, \"<form>signup</form>\")})\n77\t    d = Delegate(store, scope=ActorScope(list(allowed), creation_cap=cap),\n78\t                 vault=vault or _vault(), engine=engine, classifier=FakeCls(), trusted_pubkey=OP)\n79\t    return d, engine\n80\t\n81\t\n82\tdef _acts(engine):\n83\t    return [c for c in engine.calls if c[0] == \"act\"]\n84\t\n85\t\n86\t# ---- G2 WARDEN honesty (real oracle) -------------------------------------------------------------\n87\tdef test_warden_locks_actor_verbs_a3():\n88\t    if not _KERNEL.exists():\n89\t        print(\"    (skip real oracle — kernel not built)\")\n90\t        return\n91\t    from sigil.agents.kernel_classify clean read-verb is A0\"\n96\t\n97\t\n98\t# ---- impersonation impossible by CONSTRUCTION (absence of a code path, not a check) --------------\n99\tdef test_no_impersonation_field_exists():\n100\t
