\"*\") is False, \"DELEGATE is never promoted\"\n112\t\n113\t\n114\t# ---- scope: deny-all origin allowlist + SSRF (private host) --------------------------------------\n115\tdef test_offlist_origin_is_refused_no_request():\n116\t    s = _store()\n117\t    d, engine = _delegate(s, pages={\"https://evil.example/x\": (200, \"<html>ok</html>\")})\n118\t    queued, res = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"login\", \"https://evil.example/x\", {\"u\": \"vault:username\"})])\n119\t    assert queued == [] and any(\"REFUSED\" in n for n in res.notes), \"an off-allowlist origin is refused\"\n120\t    assert not _acts(engine), \"a refused step performs no action\"\n121\t\n122\t\n123\tdef test_private_host_is_refused_ssrf():\n124\t    s = _store()\n125\t    d, engine = _delegate(s, allowed=(\"http://127.0.0.1\",),\n126\t                          pages={\"http://127.0.0.1/login\": (200, \"<form/>\")})\n127\t    queued, res = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"login\", \"http://127.0.0.1/login\", {\"u\": \"vault:username\"})])\n128\t    assert queued == [] and any(\"REFUSED\" in n for n in res.notes), \"a private/loopback host is refused (SSRF)\"\n129\t    assert not _acts(engine)\n130\t\n131\t\n132\t# ---- the core approval gate: no approval → no request; approval → exactly one act ----------------\n133\tdef test_unapproved_step_makes_no_request():\n134\t    s = _store()\n135\t    d, engine = _delegate(s)\n136\t    queued, _ = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"login\", f\"{SVC}/signup\",\n137\t                                   {\"user\": \"vault:username\", \"pw\": \"vault:password\"})])\n138\t    assert len(queued) == 1, \"the login step queues (A3)\"\n139\t    ex = d.execute(queued[0])                          # NO approval\n140\t    assert not ex.applied and any(\"no verified owner approval\" in n for n in ex.notes)\n141\t    assert not _acts(engine), \"an unapproved step performs ZERO requests\"\n142\t\n143\t\n144\tdef test_approved_step_executes_exactly_one_act():\n145\t    s = _store()\n146\t    d, engine = _delegate(s)\n147\t    queued, _ = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"login\", f\"{SVC}/signup\",\n148\t                                   {\"user\": \"vault:username\", \"pw\": \"vault:password\"})])\n149\t    ApprovalQueue(s, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(queued[0])\n150\t    ex = d.execute(queued[0])\n151\t    assert ex.applied, \"an approved step executes\"\n152\t    acts = _acts(engine)\n153\t    assert len(acts) == 1 and sorted(acts[0][2]) == [\"pw\", \"user\"], \"exactly one act, carrying the field NAMES only\"\n154\t\n155\t\n156\tdef test_credential_never_on_spine_or_journal():\n157\t    s = _store()\n158\t    d, engine = _delegate(s)\n159\t    queued, _ = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"login\", f\"{SVC}/signup\",\n160\t                                   {\"user\": \"vault:username\", \"pw\": \"vault:password\"})])\n161\t    ApprovalQueue(s, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(queued[0])\n162\t    d.execute(queued[0])\n163\t    spine_bytes = Path(s.path).read_text()\n164\t    assert SENTINEL not in spine_bytes, \"the password NEVER appears in the append-only spine\"\n165\t    journal_bytes = \"\".join(p.read_text() for p in (actor_mod._JOURNAL).glob(\"*.json\"))\n166\t    assert SENTINEL not in journal_bytes, \"the password NEVER appears in the 0700 step journal (only a vault reference)\"\n167\t    assert not any(SENTINEL in str(c) for c in engine.calls), \"the password NEVER appears in a recorded engine call\"\n168\t\n169\t\n170\t# ---- blocks are surfaced, never defeated, never escalated ----------------------------------------\n171\tdef test_captcha_or_403_at_preview_surfaces_and_never_queues():\n172\t    for status, html in ((403, \"\"), (200, \"<div>Please complete the reCAPTCHA</div>\"), (429, \"\")):\n173\t        s = _store()\n174\t        d, engine = _delegate(s, pages={f\"{SVC}/signup\": (status, html)})\n175\t        queued, res = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"account.create\", f\"{SVC}/signup\", {\"u\": \"vault:username\"})])\n176\t        assert queued == [], f\"a blocked page ({status}) is NOT queued for action\"\n177\t        assert any(\"BLOCKED\" in n for n in res.notes)\n178\t        assert any(r.payload.get(\"signal\") == \"web.actor.blocked\" for r in s.iter_records()), \"the block is surfaced\"\n179\t        assert not _acts(engine), \"a block is never acted on (never defeated)\"\n180\t\n181\t\n182\tdef test_block_appearing_at_execute_stops_before_acting():\n183\t    s = _store()\n184\t    d, engine = _delegate(s)                            # good page at preview\n185\t    queued, _ = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"login\", f\"{SVC}/signup\", {\"pw\": \"vault:password\"})])\n186\t    ApprovalQueue(s, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(queued[0])\n187\t    engine.pages[f\"{SVC}/signup\"] = (403, \"\")           # the site starts blocking AFTER approval\n188\t    ex = d.execute(queued[0])\n189\t    assert not ex.applied and any(\"BLOCKED at execute\" in n for n in ex.notes)\n190\t    assert not _acts(engine), \"a block detected at execute stops before any action\"\n191\t\n192\t\n193\t# ---- TOCTOU: a changed page OR a rotated credential invalidates the approval ----------------------\n194\tdef test_page_change_between_approval_and_execute_aborts():\n195\t    s = _store()\n196\t    d, engine = _delegate(s)\n197\t    queued, _ = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"login\", f\"{SVC}/signup\", {\"pw\": \"vault:password\"})])\n198\t    ApprovalQueue(s, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(queued[0])\n199\t    engine.pages[f\"{SVC}/signup\"] = (200, \"<form>DIFFERENT PAGE</form>\")   # page changed\n200\t    ex = d.execute(queued[0])\n201\t    assert not ex.applied and any(\"token mismatch\" in n for n in ex.notes), \"a page change aborts (anti-TOCTOU)\"\n202\t    assert not _acts(engine)\n203\t\n204\t\n205\tdef test_credential_rotation_between_approval_and_execute_aborts():\n206\t    s = _store()\n207\t    v = _vault()\n208\t    d, engine = _delegate(s, vault=v)\n209\t    queued, _ = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"login\", f\"{SVC}/signup\", {\"pw\": \"vault:password\"})])\n210\t    ApprovalQueue(s, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(queued[0])\n211\t    v.bump(\"svc\")                                       # the owner rotated the password → version++\n212\t    ex = d.execute(queued[0])\n213\t    assert not ex.applied and any(\"token mismatch\" in n for n in ex.notes), \\\n214\t        \"a credential rotation (version bump) invalidates the approval — re-approval required\"\n215\t    assert not _acts(engine)\n216\t\n217\t\n218\tdef test_approval_of_one_step_cannot_authorize_another():\n219\t    s = _store()\n220\t    d, engine = _delegate(s)\n221\t    step = WebStep(\"svc\", \"login\", f\"{SVC}/signup\", {\"pw\": \"vault:password\"})\n222\t    queued, _ = d.preview([step, step])                # two identical steps → seqs A and B (same token)\n223\t    a, b = queued\n224\t    ApprovalQueue(s, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(a)\n225\t    assert not d.execute(b).applied, \"an approval bound to seq A cannot authorize seq B (replay refused)\"\n226\t    assert not _acts(engine), \"the replayed step performs no action\"\n227\t    assert d.execute(a).applied and len(_acts(engine)) == 1, \"the genuinely-approved step executes once\"\n228\t\n229\t\n230\t# ---- mass-creation is out of doctrine: per-service cap -------------------------------------------\n231\tdef test_per_service_creation_cap():\n232\t    s = _store()\n233\t    d, engine = _delegate(s, cap=1)\n234\t    q1, _ = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"account.create\", f\"{SVC}/signup\", {\"u\": \"vault:username\"})])\n235\t    ApprovalQueue(s, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(q1[0])\n236\t    assert d.execute(q1[0]).applied, \"the first account.create is allowed\"\n237\t    q2, res = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"account.create\", f\"{SVC}/signup\", {\"u\": \"vault:username\"})])\n238\t    assert q2 == [] and any(\"creation cap\" in n for n in res.notes), \"a second account.create is refused (cap=1)\"\n239\t\n240\t\n241\t# ---- construction: the actor has NO browser/escalation path (block-before-escalate is vacuous) ---\n242\tdef test_actor_has_no_browser_escalation_path():\n243\t    src = Path(actor_mod.__file__).read_text()\n244\t    # no browser/JS-render/escalation call path (impersonation is covered structurally by the field test)\n245\t    for banned in (\"BrowserEngine\", \"needs_js\", \"playwright\", \"escalate\", \".act(\", \"import subprocess\"):\n246\t        if banned == \".act(\":\n247\t            assert src.count(\".act(\") == 1, \"exactly one engine.act call site (the single, gated action)\"\n248\t            continue\n249\t        assert banned not in src, f\"the actor must contain no {banned!r} path (HTTP-only, no evasion surface)\"\n250\t\n251\t\n252\t# ---- the REAL HttpEngine independently refuses a private host (defense-in-depth below {e}\")\n271\t            traceback.print_exc()\n272\t    print(f\"{passed}/{len(fns)} Phase-8 WS-G (DELEGATE) doctrine guarantees {"parentUuid":"6cc47a49-e777-465b-be8c-3c4b983e4329","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a5de18e8326611e83","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAHXhnMCMPFpXyQSKbi3","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01GnXZ8mME8Hik2EHBoxyc1w","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /home/kali/sigil && echo \"=== platform/secrets.py ===\" && cat sigil/platform/secrets.py 2>/dev/null | head -80; echo \"=== authorized_devices ===\"; sed -n '1,140p' sigil/mesh/registry.py | grep -n \"def authorized_devices\" ; sed -n '/def authorized_devices/,/^def /p' sigil/mesh/registry.py; echo \"=== kernel binary ===\"; ls -la /home/kali/sigil/kernel/target/release/sigil-kernel 2>&1 | head","description":"Read SecretStore, authorized_devices, kernel presence"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":9080,"cache_read_input_tokens":64580,"output_tokens":10571,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":9080},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":10571,"cache_read_input_tokens":64580,"cache_creation_input_tokens":9080,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":9080,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAHXfCKcU14uRTP1GYyy","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"f2967653-3488-428c-966a-32812a85f9b2","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:11:15.642Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"f2967653-3488-428c-966a-32812a85f9b2","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a5de18e8326611e83","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GnXZ8mME8Hik2EHBoxyc1w","type":"tool_result","content":"=== platform/secrets.py ===\n\"\"\"SecretStore (Phase 7, WS-D D-ii) — keyring-first secret handling. Reads/writes API keys via the\nOS keyring (macOS Keychain / Windows Credential Manager / libsecret) when the `keyring` package is\npresent; otherwise falls back to the plaintext `~/.sigil/sigil.env` that `config._load_env_file`\nalready loads. Secrets NEVER enter the append-only spine, a log, or a network payload — this is\nowner-local plumbing that keeps them off the plaintext file and off the wire (WS-D transport carries\nonly `{seq,tier,kind}`).\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n\n_SERVICE = \"sigil\"\n\n\nclass SecretStore:\n    def __init__(self):\n        try:\n            import keyring\n            self._kr = keyring\n        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — no keyring backend → env fallback only\n            self._kr = None\n\n    @property\n    def backend(self) -> str:\n        return \"keyring\" if self._kr is not None else \"envfile\"\n\n    def get(self, key: str) -> Optional[str]:\n        if self._kr is not None:\n            try:\n                v = self._kr.get_password(_SERVICE, key)\n                if v is not None:\n                    return v\n            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n                pass\n        return os.environ.get(key)                    # sigil.env is loaded into env at config import\n\n    def set(self, key: str, value: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Store a secret. Returns the backend used. Prefers the keyring; falls back to sigil.env\n        (0600). Never writes the value anywhere else.\"\"\"\n        if self._kr is not None:\n            try:\n                self._kr.set_password(_SERVICE, key, value)\n                os.environ[key] = value               # make it live this process too\n                return \"keyring\"\n            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n                pass\n        self._env_upsert(key, value)\n        os.environ[key] = value\n        return \"envfile\"\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _env_upsert(key: str, value: str) -> None:\n        f = SIGIL_HOME / \"sigil.env\"\n        SIGIL_HOME.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        lines = []\n        found = False\n        try:\n            for ln in f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines():\n                if ln.split(\"=\", 1)[0].strip() == key:\n                    lines.append(f\"{key}={value}\"); found = True\n                else:\n                    lines.append(ln)\n        except OSError:\n            pass\n        if not found:\n            lines.append(f\"{key}={value}\")\n        # create with 0600 up-front (no world-readable window on first write) — N4\n        fd = os.open(str(f), os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)\n        with os.fdopen(fd, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as fh:\n            fh.write(\"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\")\n=== authorized_devices ===\n66:def authorized_devices(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> Set[str]:\ndef authorized_devices(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> Set[str]:\n    \"\"\"The set of currently-authorized device pubkeys (owner-signed authorize, minus later revoke).\n    An unsigned/forged authorization is ignored — a rogue device cannot self-authorize.\"\"\"\n    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n    state: dict = {}\n    for r in store.iter_records():\n        p = r.payload\n        if p.get(\"signal\") == DEV_SIGNAL and p.get(\"state\") in (\"authorized\", \"revoked\") \\\n                and verify_signed(p, _DEV_CORE, tp):\n            state[p.get(\"device_pubkey\")] = p[\"state\"]      # latest verified state per device key\n    return {pub for pub, st in state.items() if st == \"authorized\"}\n\n\nclass DeviceApprover:\n    \"\"\"Signs an approval with a DEVICE key (the phone). The record verifies via\n    `verify_approval(record, owner_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices(store))` — i.e. only while\n    the device remains authorized. Never touches the owner trust-root key.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, store: SpineStore, *, device_key):\n        self.store = store\n        self.device_key = device_key\n\n    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, approver: str, reason: str) -> int:\n        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n        sig = sign(self.device_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n        payload = {\"signal\": _APPROVAL_SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq,\n                   \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason, \"pubkey\": self.device_key.public_key_b64,\n                   \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg), \"device\": True, \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\", payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n\n    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver, reason)\n\n    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver, reason)\n=== kernel binary ===\n-rwxrwxr-x 2 kali kali 1566408 Jul 18 17:19 /home/kali/sigil/kernel/target/release/sigil-kernel","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"7a9daf4b-fcbf-4b12-868c-b893a3f7f834","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:11:18.031Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f2967653-3488-428c-966a-32812a85f9b2","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

===\ndef action_token(service: str, step_kind: str, page_sha256: str, field_binding: str) -> str:\n    return sha256_hex(f\"{service}|{step_kind}|{page_sha256}|{field_binding}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n=== execute: token components re-derived (url NOT among service|kind|page|fb) ===\n        # RE-DERIVE the token from the CURRENT page hash + CURRENT vault version + journaled fields; a\n        # page change OR a credential rotation OR a journal edit → mismatch → abort (never trust a field).\n        fresh = action_token(service, kind, sha256_hex(pv.html.encode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\")),\n                             self._field_binding(service, fields))\n        if fresh != token:\n            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"target_seq\": step_seq,\n                                       \"reason\": \"page/credential/step changed since approval (token mismatch) — aborted\"})\n            res.notes.append(\"ABORTED: the world changed since approval (token mismatch) — no action\")\n            return res\n        resolved = self._resolve(service, fields)            # credentials → locals ONLY (never the spine)\n        out = self.engine.act({\"url\": url, \"kind\": kind}, resolved)\n=== actor_scope docstring claim ===\nPlus a per-service `creation_cap` (default 1) — mass account creation is out of doctrine; the cap is\nchecked at preview AND execute.\"\"\"","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"228fb704-c511-47a6-a6cc-df7acc65d50f","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:14:59.942Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"10dae6f8-1e02-455f-8d9c-da6c6dc095a3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

The owner approves **seq0**, whose spine record shows `url = /login`. `execute(seq0)` → re-derived token matches (identical HTML), no abort → **the credential POST fires at `/promo`, not the approved `/login`.** Observed: `APPROVED /login BUT FIRED AT /promo`.\n- Impact bound (stated honestly): reachable **same-origin** with no tampering, because an off-allowlist second URL is refused at preview and never journaled.
